## Structural diagnosis and data audit

Origin was fetched before the function batch; git diff HEAD origin/main for the four code leaves was empty. Initial ctxdiff results: Zi8MatchROMdata1 128/128 instructions, 5 differences, only r26/r27 allocation; Zi8GetDataSignature 69/69, 9 differences, destination/language/signature saved registers; Zi8IsDupWChar 63/63, 8 differences, character/duplicate allocation; Getter_ 609/609, 14 differences, security-buffer/index and country-loop registers; VIInit 338/338, 14 differences in inlined AdjustPosition and the shared zero constant; __VIRetraceHandler 516/516, 11 differences in inlined getCurrentHalfLine. Stack frames, branches, and helper boundaries agree in all six baseline functions. Initial position-callback experiments in retrace changed the wrong inline region and were discarded; target disassembly localized the difference to VISetRegs -> getCurrentFieldEvenOdd -> getCurrentHalfLine.

Declaration searches: zi8dawg leading locals, 2 builds, best 0 structural/5 exact differences; signature leading locals, 2 builds, best 0/9; duplicate leading locals, 6 builds, best 0/8; VIInit leading locals, 36 builds, best 0/14. No changes retained.

MyTiManager data: all 91 functions are already exact. Target .data 984 bytes, compiled .data 944 bytes. Every named vtable has the same offset, size and relocation sequence; Manager is 320 bytes at 0x270, final entries at 0x3a0, 0x3a4, 0x3a8, 0x3ac relocate to createMemoInputForm/createLetterInputForm/createBigTextInputForm/createBG. Target lbl_816681E8 is 40 zero bytes beginning at 0x3b0, without relocations, after that table. No type or incoming relocation proves a real owner, so neither its extent nor the split boundary is changed. Target .sdata positions at 0, 8, 16 correspond to source hidden/appearing/letter VEC2 constants, but .sdata already scores 100 and renaming adds no match. No speculative rename. .bss, .ctors, .sbss, .sdata, .sdata2 already score 100. Other four owned units already have 100 percent data, including exception tables, switch tables and literal pools; no data metadata changes are needed.

Accepted retrace candidate: getCurrentHalfLine declares verticalCount, previousVerticalCount, horizontalCount, and sums the vertical factor before the horizontal quotient. This produces 516/516 instructions, ctxdiff 0. Quick full build/DOL gate passed; vi improves 28 -> 29 instruction-exact functions, code 7880 -> 9944, data stays 1944; zero regressions and zero forbidden/readability findings. Clean full gate pending before commit.

## Final open-function audit

The first clean full gate over all five leaves passed, with the retail DOL SHA1 26116613f624061ba99c8d1a299aaa6efa85670d, zero regressions, zero forbidden additions, and zero readability findings. Commit 72f78211 preserves the exact retrace result and the initial attempts record. Later trials retained no additional source changes.

Every remaining function has at least three distinct executed source-level attempts in this log: Zi8MatchROMdata1 7, Zi8GetDataSignature 10, Zi8IsDupWChar 14, Getter_ 11, VIInit 11. The failed C89 mid-block declaration is included in the record but is not needed to meet that minimum. Declaration-order trials and helper-boundary trials are separate from type, expression and control-flow changes. All unsuccessful source variants were restored, and all build products will be recreated by the final clean gate.

Remaining: Zi8MatchROMdata1 99.765625%, 128/128 instructions, 5 saved-register differences; Zi8GetDataSignature 99.347824%, 69/69, 9 saved-register differences; Zi8IsDupWChar 99.36508%, 63/63, 8 character/duplicate register differences; Getter_ 99.86043%, 609/609, 14 security-buffer/country-index register differences; VIInit 99.7929%, 338/338, 14 inline clipping/shared-zero register differences. No fuzzy-only source experiment is retained. MyTiManager's 40-byte relocation-free zero tail remains unproven; no padding, forced object, speculative data label or section boundary change was added.

Final clean full gate: GATE PASS. All five unit pools IDENTICAL. DOL SHA1 26116613f624061ba99c8d1a299aaa6efa85670d. Zero regressions, forbidden additions and readability findings. Instruction-exact counts MyTiManager 91 -> 91, zi8dawg 5 -> 5, www_wiisetting 20 -> 20, zi8getc2 15 -> 15, vi 28 -> 29. Matched code bytes 20196 -> 20196, 4356 -> 4356, 3740 -> 3740, 6360 -> 6360, 7880 -> 9944; matched data bytes unchanged 148, 120, 3856, 332, 1944 respectively.

Data audit reconfirmed: eight units have full matched data already, with no renames needed. MyTiManager remains 148/1132: its sole section mismatch is a relocation-free 40-byte zero tail after the exact 320-byte Manager vtable. Ownership is not proven, so no symbol extent, address, split, or section total was changed.

set_err_msg structural evidence: target carries r30 from the prefix getMessage return through formatting; its default error switch does not assign a message ID before passing r30 into the final getMessage. Source initializes the default ID to MESG_ERROR_CODE and therefore has one extra instruction. Removing the initialization would introduce an uninitialized ID and is forbidden; every tested initialized branch variant is restored. The intended original default behavior remains uncertain.

onEvent__Q33ipl5scene12AddressEventFUlUlPv is objdiff 100%, 161/161 instructions.
